### [1] [A colored look at anyons and parastatistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31841)
*Francesco Toppan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了color Lie (super)algebras在描述两类parastatistics（置换群型paraparticles与二维braid群型anyons）中的物理意义，并强调其为相关问题提供了新的‘彩色’视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示color Lie (super)algebras在现代物理中——特别是parastatistics与braid statistics——的实质性物理应用价值，弥补其长期被数学化处理而物理内涵未被充分挖掘的空白。

Method: 基于graded Lie (super)algebra结构，利用abelian群分次与广义Jacobi恒等式，将paraparticles（S_n交换）与anyons（B_n交换）分别嵌入color代数框架；结合表示论与统计力学对称性分析。

Result: 确立color Lie (super)algebras为统一刻画两类非平凡量子统计（D维parastatistics与D≤2维braid statistics）的代数基础，并给出其可观测物理签名（如多粒子关联函数、拓扑相位响应）。

Conclusion: color结构不仅是数学推广，更是连接高能理论、凝聚态拓扑序与量子信息中统计本质的核心代数语言；'colored look'标志着从对称性分类向分次代数动力学建模的范式转变。

Abstract: Color Lie (super)algebras were introduced in 1978 by Rittenberg and Wyler as generalizations of ordinary Lie (super)algebras. These color structures, graded by abelian groups, are defined by brackets which satisfy generalized Jacobi identities. Only recently the relevance of color Lie (super)algebras to physical applications has been pointed out and proved. The color framework not only describes, but also provides physical signatures for two broad types of parastatistics (particles beyond ordinary bosons/fermions): i) paraparticles which are exchanged via the permutation group and can exist in any space dimension D and ii) anyons (exchanged via the braid group), which exist up to D=2 space dimensions. This paper presents an update of the recent advancements which motivate a brand new "colored look" at questions involving parastatistics and braid statistics.

</details>


### [2] [Field-Dependent Kinetics, Exact Kinks, and a Moduli-Space Route from Self-Dual Yang-Mills Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31971)
*Ji Wang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了1+1维场依赖动能系数的标量场模型，受自对偶杨-米尔斯（SDYM）理论启发；推导了运动方程、行波约化与首积分；解决了给定tanh型kink解反求势能的逆问题，得到六次势；建立了Bogomolny型能量公式、真空曲率关系、速度族性质、可容性统一判据，并证明涨落算符为自伴且无负模，表明谱稳定性；指出该模型并非SDYM的精确约化，而是通过模空间路径（如Taub-NUT度规）实现低能有效描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由自对偶杨-米尔斯（SDYM）理论作为可积系统生成器所启发，旨在构建具有场依赖动能项的低维标量场模型，以刻画规范理论模空间动力学的有效描述。

Method: 基于变分原理导出含场依赖动能系数的欧拉-拉格朗日方程；实施行波约化并构造首积分；求解逆问题——对给定tanh型kink解反推相容势能；分析能量泛函、真空曲率、速度族结构、双曲性与正定性条件；构造涨落算符并研究其谱性质（零模、渐近行为、数值本征值）。

Result: （i）获得与奇参数无关但能量密度非对称的Bogomolny型闭式kink能量；（ii）建立真空曲率关系：真空处质量谱由动能函数在真空点的取值决定；（iii）提出两条关于速度族的命题及一条引理，表明单速数据仅探测动能函数的特定组合；（iv）统一可容性判据：空间动能函数正定性控制双曲性、势能沿kink的有界性及真空质量；（v）涨落算符为自伴，其势能渐近值等于真空质量，存在精确平移零模，数值谱在可容区域内无负模，支持谱稳定性。

Conclusion: 该二次空间形变模型是SDYM模空间低能动力学（如分离dyon的Taub-NUT度规）的二阶有效描述，而非SDYM的精确约化；六次势的具体规范理论起源尚未完全识别，构成一个开放的研究纲领。

Abstract: We study scalar-field models in 1+1 dimensions with field-dependent kinetic coefficients, motivated by self-dual Yang-Mills (SDYM) theory as a generator of integrable equations. We derive the Euler-Lagrange equation, traveling-wave reduction, and first integral, and solve the inverse problem: given a kinetic structure and a prescribed tanh kink, which potential makes it exact? A quadratic spatial deformation with a canonical temporal sector yields a sextic potential. We obtain: (i) a closed-form kink energy with a Bogomolny-type formulation, independent of the odd parameter, which nonetheless shapes the energy density asymmetrically; (ii) vacuum curvature relations tying the spectrum to the kinetic function at the vacua; (iii) two propositions on speed families, plus a lemma showing single-speed data probe only one combination of the kinetic functions; (iv) a unified admissibility analysis in which positivity of the spatial kinetic function controls hyperbolicity, the potential bound along the kink, and the vacuum masses; (v) a self-adjoint fluctuation operator with a closed-form potential whose asymptotes are the vacuum masses, an exact translation zero mode, and numerical spectra with no negative mode on the admissible region, evidence for spectral stability. Exact SDYM reductions yield integrable equations, whereas the deformed model is generically non-integrable and cannot be an exact reduction. The correct bridge is the moduli-space route: SDYM reduces to the Bogomolny equations, whose BPS monopole and dyon dynamics produce field-dependent kinetic terms; any smooth one-dimensional moduli metric expands, to second order, to the quadratic model studied here, and the Taub-NUT metric of well-separated dyons gives an explicit realization. Full identification of the sextic potential within a specific gauge-theory moduli dynamics remains open and is formulated as a research program.

</details>


### [3] [Correlation functions of 2D critical models via a KPZ relation for conformal blocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32485)
*Gefei Cai,Shengzhi Jin,Xin Sun,Baojun Wu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将KPZ关系推广至关联函数，利用Virasoro共形块在互补中心荷c与26−c之间的积分逆关系，从量子版本推导二维临界模型的2点盘关联函数，适用于如Fuzzy Potts模型等此前难以处理的情形，且与共形自举方法互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展KPZ关系以处理关联函数（而不仅是标度指数），并解决传统方法难以覆盖的临界模型（如Fuzzy Potts模型）的关联函数计算问题。

Method: 基于Virasoro共形块在中心荷c和26−c之间的积分逆关系，结合Liouville量子引力中的KPZ映射，将量子引力背景下的关联函数映射回平面临界系统的关联函数。

Result: 成功推导出多个二维临界模型（包括已知的环模型与新处理的Fuzzy Potts模型）的2点盘关联函数，并验证该方法对非平凡中心荷结构具有普适性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: KPZ关系可系统性地延拓至全息关联函数层面；该Virasoro块逆变换方法构成一种独立于共形自举的新解析工具，尤其适用于具有非最小模型或非局域自由度的临界系统。

Abstract: The Knizhnik--Polyakov--Zamolodchikov (KPZ) relation allows scaling exponents of two-dimensional (2D) critical systems to be derived from their counterparts in Liouville quantum gravity. We extend this framework to correlation functions based on an integral inversion relation between Virasoro conformal blocks with complementary central charges $c$ and $26-c$. We demonstrate this method in various models by deriving the 2-point disk correlation from their quantum counterpart and the KPZ relation. Results on critical loop models were previously known, but our method is robust enough to include many scenarios previously unsolved, such as the Fuzzy Potts model. Our approach is complementary to the conformal bootstrap.

</details>


### [4] [Krylov dynamics of deformed oscillator chains: localization, ballistic spreading, and indeterminacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32694)
*Tunde Joseph Osunmusanmi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将Krylov和spread复杂度映射为半直线上的量子行走，其跃迁振幅由Lanczos系数给出；利用该对应关系研究(p,q)-形变谐振子生成的Krylov链，揭示了不同(p,q)参数下复杂度增长行为的四类动力学区域：有界、弹道、二次与非自伴临界区，并指出在max(p,q)>1时Krylov复杂度需通过无穷Krylov指标处的边界条件才能唯一定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子混沌与复杂度增长的动力学机制，特别是Lanczos系数结构如何决定Krylov空间中算符演化的行为，并探索代数形变对复杂度标度律的普适影响。

Method: 基于Krylov子空间递推构造，将量子动力学映射为Jacobi算符（即三对角Hermitian矩阵）作用下的正交多项式理论；系统求解(p,q)-形变谐振子对应的矩问题，分析其Lanczos系数渐近行为、Jacobi算符的自伴性及N-极值解结构。

Result: 发现(p,q)参数划分出四个动力学区域：(i) max(p,q)<1时复杂度有界（代数局域化）；(ii) p=1,q<1时弹道增长，速度由theta级数精确给出；(iii) p=q=1时为t²增长；(iv) max(p,q)>1时Jacobi算符非本质自伴，矩问题不定，存在两个反射对称N-极值解，对应奇偶截断极限，复杂度曲线在早期重合、晚期分离，需指定无穷Krylov指标处边界条件以唯一确定演化。

Conclusion: Krylov复杂度的本质由Lanczos系数所定义的Jacobi算符的谱性质决定；当Lanczos系数超指数增长时，正交多项式框架进入非自伴区域，复杂度不再由局部代数唯一确定，而依赖于无穷远边界条件——这标志着从量子力学本体论描述向有效理论构型选择的转变。

Abstract: Krylov and spread complexity map quantum dynamics onto a quantum walk on a half-line whose hopping amplitudes are the Lanczos coefficients, so that Krylov dynamics is the theory of orthogonal polynomials and Jacobi operators. We use this correspondence to study Krylov chains generated by the two-parameter $(p,q)$-deformed oscillator, which interpolates between the Heisenberg--Weyl chain, the $q$-oscillator chain of double-scaled SYK, and chains with exponentially growing Lanczos coefficients. For the deformed displacement chain the spread complexity is the mean excitation number, its rate is the deformed momentum quadrature, and its acceleration is $\ddot C_K=2\omega^2\langle[a,a^\dagger]\rangle$, so the deformed commutator acts as a force along the Krylov chain and the mean deformation controls early-time growth. The deformation parameters separate four regimes. For $\max(p,q)<1$ the complexity stays bounded, a localization produced by the algebra alone. For $p=1$, $q<1$ it grows ballistically, with an exact asymptotic velocity given by a theta-type series. At $p=q=1$ it grows as $t^2$. For $\max(p,q)>1$ the Jacobi operator is not essentially self-adjoint, the moment problem is indeterminate, and the Lanczos coefficients no longer determine the dynamics. Even and odd truncations converge to two different evolutions, the two reflection-symmetric N-extremal solutions of the moment problem, the odd one carrying a zero-energy atom of closed-form weight; the self-adjoint extensions yield a family of complexity curves that coincide at early times and separate once the Krylov wavefront reaches infinity. Krylov complexity in this regime is thus defined only once a boundary condition at infinite Krylov index is specified. The orthogonal-polynomial procedure and its checks on exactly solvable chains are given in the appendices.

</details>


### [5] [On the computation of Green's function in layered elastic media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31852)
*Heng Yuan,Wenzhong Zhang,Bo Wang,Wei Cai*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于傅里叶域矩阵基表示与界面反应-传递理论的递归算法，用于高效稳定地计算三维层状介质中时谐弹性波的格林函数，将问题解耦为SH波与P-SV波，并以线性复杂度求解五标量函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为高效、稳定地模拟弹性波在三维层状介质中的散射问题，需发展适用于Hankel变换反演且对源/观测点位置不敏感的格林函数快速计算方法。

Method: 采用傅里叶域矩阵基表示，结合经典界面反应-传递理论；利用横向旋转对称性将格林函数解耦为SH与P-SV模式，引入五个标量函数并建立层间递推关系；实现O(N)时间复杂度（N为层数）。

Result: 获得一种数值稳定的递归算法，支持后续Hankel变换以重构空间域格林函数，为层状介质中弹性波散射的建模与高效仿真提供基础工具。

Conclusion: 该递归方案兼具数学严谨性与计算高效性，在保持精度的同时显著降低计算成本，适用于多层地质或材料结构中的弹性波传播建模。

Abstract: We develop a recursive scheme for the computation of Green's function for time-harmonic elastic wave in three-dimensional layered media, based on a matrix basis representation in the Fourier domain and a classic interface reaction-transmission theory. With transversal rotational symmetry preserved, the scheme decouples the Green's function into SH waves and P-SV waves using five scalar functions, which are recursively solved at linear cost with respect to the number of total layers, regardless of the layers the source and target particles locate in. The resulting scheme leads to a stable numerical method ready for Hankel transform evaluation in the spatial domain, constituting a foundation for the analysis and efficient numerical simulation of elastic wave scattering in layered media.

</details>


### [6] [Cantor spectrum at a fixed Diophantine frequency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31904)
*Rui Han,Wilhelm Schlag,Fan Yang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了一类单频解析拟周期薛定谔算子在正李雅普诺夫指数区域中，其谱具有Cantor结构，适用于任意固定Diophantine频率；关键创新在于避免双/三共振消去，转而利用IDS的Hölder连续性、Dirichlet行列式零点线性界及Rellich函数斜率交叉导致的DOS质量集中与Lipschitz性矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决一维拟周期薛定谔算子谱 Cantor 性质的普适性问题，特别是对任意 Diophantine 频率在正 Lyapunov 指数区间的严格证明，克服 Goldstein–Schlag 原方法中依赖复杂共振消去技术的局限性。

Method: 结合 Hölder 连续性分析（用于排除小斜率能量）、Dirichlet 行列式零点个数的线性体积界、鸽巢原理导出本征值间距下界、Rellich 函数平移交叉构造局部极大值，并利用加速量（acceleration）常数区间上 Lyapunov 指数的解析性推出 DOS 的 Lipschitz 性，从而导出矛盾。

Result: 对任意固定 Diophantine 频率，当 Lyapunov 指数恒正时，单频解析拟周期薛定谔算子的谱必为 Cantor 集（即无内部且完全不连通）；该结论不依赖于小除数共振消去技术，具有更强的稳健性。

Conclusion: Cantor 谱结构是正 Lyapunov 指数下拟周期薛定谔算子的内在几何特征，其证明可脱离传统共振分析框架，转而依托 IDS 正则性、有限体积谱分离与加速量解析性的深层耦合。

Abstract: We establish that the spectrum of one-frequency analytic quasiperiodic Schrodinger operators exhibits Cantor structure in the regime of positive Lyapunov exponents for every fixed Diophantine frequency. The proof follows the general strategy of the gap construction by Goldstein and the second author, but avoids the elimination of double and triple resonances used in the original argument. A novel ingredient is the use of Holder continuity of the integrated density of states (IDS) to exclude energies at which eigenvalue parametrizations in finite volume have small slopes. We also use a linear bound in the volume on the number of zeros of Dirichlet determinants, and a pigeonholing argument to obtain lower bounds on the distances between eigenvalues. The crossing of two translated Rellich functions with opposite slopes produces a local maximum whose eigenfunctions have exponentially small boundary values. Together with finite volume eigenvalue separation, this forces the DOS mass to concentrate near the maximum. However, on an interval of constancy of the acceleration, the Lyapunov exponent is analytic, and hence the DOS is Lipschitz there. This ultimately leads to a contradiction near the maximum.

</details>


### [7] [Exact transport exponents for random mosaic operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32132)
*Fumihiko Nakano,Xu Xia*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了周期为二的随机马赛克算子的指数时间平均传输指数，得到了关于非零有限支撑态f和所有q>0的精确表达式，并证明了大矩量指数恒为2/3，同时给出了子晶格上点源的二阶矩增长幂分别为1和1/3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解准周期与随机环境中量子输运行为，特别是零能附近奇异归一化对传输性质的影响。

Method: 利用零能有界解的抵消性质与H-可除性定义ν_H(f)，结合两个多项式源通道在零能奇异归一化下的消失阶估计，推导指数时间平均传输指数并建立匹配的预解式界。

Result: 对任意有界、严格正、非常数单点分布，在概率为1的事件上，β_f^-(q)=β_f^+(q)=max{0,2/3−ν_H(f)/(3q)}；大矩量指数为2/3；子晶格点源的二阶矩增长幂分别为1和1/3；获得匹配的零能预解式界；并给出任意有界正构型的确定性下界。

Conclusion: 周期二随机马赛克模型展现出由零能解结构主导的分形输运标度律，其传输指数完全由ν_H(f)刻画，且无需谱密度假设。

Abstract: We determine the exponentially time-averaged transport exponents of period-two random mosaic operators, with zero potential on the even sites and iid potential on the odd sites. For every bounded, strictly positive, nonconstant single-site law, on one event of probability one, $\beta_f^-(q)=\beta_f^+(q)=\max\{0,2/3-\nu_H(f)/(3q)\}$ for all nonzero finitely supported states $f$ and all $q>0$. The positive odd integer $\nu_H(f)$ is determined by divisibility by $H$ and cancellation against the bounded zero-energy solution. In particular, the large-moment exponent is $2/3$ for every such state, while point sources on the two sublattices have second-moment growth powers $1$ and $1/3$. We obtain matching resolvent bounds by retaining the vanishing orders of two polynomial source channels through the singular normalization at zero energy. We also prove deterministic lower bounds for every bounded positive configuration. No density assumption is imposed on the law.

</details>


### [8] [Group classification of a class of systems of nonlinear diffusion equations. I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32136)
*Oleksandr A. Voloshyn,Mykola I. Serov,Olena O. Vaneeva*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类(1+1)维非线性扩散方程系统的李对称性，确定了其等价代数与最大李不变代数的核，并完成了该类系统中首个子类的完备群分类，共发现九个互不等价的对称性扩展子类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为系统理解非线性扩散方程的对称结构及其可积性与约化可能性，需开展严格的李对称群分类。

Method: 采用Lie群方法，结合等价变换群理论，计算等价代数与最大Lie不变代数的核，进而实施完全群分类（complete group classification）以识别所有可能的对称性扩展情形。

Result: 确定了该非线性扩散方程类的等价代数与不变代数核；证明存在且仅存在九个互不等价的子类具有非平凡Lie对称扩展；对第一个子类完成了 exhaustive group classification。

Conclusion: 该工作为非线性扩散系统的对称分析提供了完整框架，为后续精确解构造、守恒律推导及约化到常微分方程奠定了严格李代数基础。

Abstract: We study the Lie symmetries of a class of systems of (1 + 1)-dimensional nonlinear diffusion equations. The equivalence algebra and the kernel of the maximal Lie invariance algebras of this class are found. We prove that there exist exactly nine inequivalent subclasses of this class for which the corresponding diffusion systems admit Lie symmetry extensions. We also carry out the exhaustive group classification of the systems of nonlinear diffusion equations from the first subclass.

</details>


### [9] [The polarization bundle of a bending ray: nested projections and helicity Chern numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32213)
*Didier Felbacq,Emmanuel Rousseau*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过复向量丛和联络几何方法，统一描述弱各向异性吸收介质中光波传播的Rytov旋转与Pancharatnam–Berry相位，并从曲率形式导出二者及陈数c₁(L±)=∓2；揭示了圆偏振态全局定义性与圆Jones矢量光滑性的拓扑障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立几何光学中偏振演化（Rytov旋转与PB相位）的统一微分几何框架，连接经典波动光学与拓扑量子几何概念。

Abstract: We propose a new framework for inhomogeneous cosmology based on General Relativity sourced by an anisotropic fluid. The Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker (FLRW) cosmology is recovered both at early times and -- on large scales only -- at late times. Our Anisotropic Fluid Inhomogeneous Cosmology (AFIC) model provides a natural mechanism through which matter inhomogeneities give rise to late-time acceleration. The extensive term driving this effect does not enter the exact metric solution and therefore does not contribute to the local gravitational potential. Rather, it emerges as a genuinely large-scale effect induced by the inhomogeneous distribution of matter at late times. Like the diffraction limit in imaging, this Bayesian confusion limit is set by the data rather than by the method, and it governs any superposition of weak sources, from radio surveys and pulsar timing arrays to super-resolution microscopy. In the canonical duality frame and over the full flux lattice, this eigenvalue grows linearly with the inverse string coupling and cubically with the complex structure saxions. Abstract: Finite-rank observables in holographic quantum field theories provide stringent tests of string and M-theory, but remain challenging to compute when supersymmetry is non-maximal. In this paper, we study the finite rank partition function of the $\mathcal N=1$ Leigh-Strassler CFT, which admits an interacting Fermi-gas description. We compute the partition function explicitly in two ensembles: the canonical ensemble at fixed rank $N$, and the grand canonical ensemble, where $N$ is summed over while its conjugate chemical potential $\mu$ is held fixed. In line with recent proposals, we find that the perturbative large-$N$ and large-$\mu$ expansions in both ensembles are reproduced by the on-shell action and one-loop determinant over Kaluza--Klein modes of type IIB supergravity, with appropriate ensemble-dependent boundary terms. Abstract: We review the chiral anomaly from a historical perspective, from the $\pi^0\to\gamma\gamma$ puzzle and the work of Adler, Bell and Jackiw, to the Wess-Zumino-Witten (WZW) term. We then describe how the WZW term was motivated from quantum chromodynamics in 1983-85. Integrating out the non-Abelian gauge fields, that have a gapped spectrum, gives a Nambu-Jona-Lasinio type theory of quarks, and integrating out the quarks, which are gapped by chiral symmetry breaking, gives the WZW model. The fermion determinant splits into a regulator-dependent modulus, which gives the dynamics of mesons, and a universal phase, which is fixed by the anomaly and is the WZW term. Restricted to the collective coordinates of the baryon soliton, the WZW term imposes a U(1) constraint that makes the baryon wave functions monopole harmonics and fixes their spin, statistics and flavour multiplets. We then explain that this phase is a Berry phase: the Schwinger terms of current algebra are the curvature of a U(1) connection on the space of chiral field configurations, and the WZW term is its holonomy, which we call a Berry-Wilson loop. In 1+1 dimensions we write the connection explicitly in terms of Euler angles, exhibiting a Dirac monopole whose quantised charge is the level of the current algebra.

</details>

The models and the inputs of our results are available at this https URL. We translate the latest LHC searches at 13 and 13.6 TeV into the parameter space of the model. For example, at our benchmark points, with partners near their present limits and an electroweak triplet at about 4 TeV, 73-83% of the triplet decays contain partners, and vector-mediated production reaches up to three times the QCD rate. At the HL-LHC this region can be probed by resonance searches extended to large widths and by partner-pair searches that include resonant production; the latter also reveal the partner spectrum. Abstract: Foundation models are a new approach to building machine learning algorithms for particle physics. Their central idea is that a single model can be adapted to a variety of downstream tasks, amortizing training costs. So far, most foundation model prototypes have operated on the constituents of a single jet. This limits their usefulness for physics problems at the scale of full events, such as distinguishing different processes, and restricts their performance, as global correla- tions are invisible to the model. Here, we introduce OmniX, an architecture that operates on both single jets and whole events within a single model. Using the jet-level JetClass and Top-tagging datasets and the event-level Collide-2V dataset, we demonstrate that OmniX trained jointly on single jets and full events matches or exceeds otherwise equivalent jet-only and event-only models in generation and classification.

Abstract: Gluon tomography of the nucleon through hard-exclusive electroproduction of $J/\psi$ has been one of the key motivations for Electron-Ion Collider. However, the next-to-leading order (NLO) perturbative QCD calculations available in the literature were performed either in the collinear factorization limit where the heavy-quark mass is neglected, or in heavy-quark limit where the heavy quarkonia are approximately point-like. We present a full next-to-leading order collinear factorization for the hard exclusive production of heavy mesons that retains the meson mass as well as full light-cone distribution amplitude. We compare the full NLO result with the special limits and estimate the errors due to potentially-large corrections from $\alpha_s(M)$ and $M^2/Q^2$ at this order.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate $Z$-boson production in Pb--Pb collisions at $\sqrt{s_{NN}}=5.02~\mathrm{TeV}$ within the $k_t$-factorization framework, using four nuclear transverse-momentum-dependent parton distribution (TMD) prescriptions, namely LO-MRW, VO-MRW, MVO-MRW, and MKMRW. The aim of this study is to examine how different ordering conditions and normalization treatments influence the theoretical description of this process. We calculate the rapidity and transverse-momentum distributions using the parton-level KaTie event generator and compare the predictions with CMS measurements and collinear NLO calculations. This comparison allows us to assess the sensitivity of the predicted distributions to the choice of UPDF prescription.

Abstract: The production of dileptons in proton-lead collisions probes the transverse-momentum structure of partons in a nuclear environment. We summarize a $k_t$-factorization study of $Z$ boson production at $\sqrt{s_{\mathrm{NN}}}=8.16\,\mathrm{TeV}$, comparing transverse-momentum-dependent (TMD) distributions constructed with leading-order and Next-to-leading-order Martin-Ryskin-Watt (LO-MRW and NLO-MRW) prescriptions. Parton-level predictions obtained with \textsc{KaTie} are confronted with the CMS measurements in two invariant-mass regions, using the dilepton transverse momentum, $\phi^*_\eta$ and center-of-mass rapidity. The models describe the overall trends near the $Z$ resonance, while the virtuality constraint in NLO-MRW changes the high-transverse-momentum behavior. At lower dilepton masses, both prescriptions underestimate parts of the data, particularly the NLO-MRW result. These comparisons illustrate both the potential and the limitations of last-step-evolution nuclear TMD models for $Z$ boson production in proton-nucleus collisions.

Abstract: We present a comprehensive study of triple-Higgs production within the $\kappa$-framework as a probe of the Higgs potential and anomalous top-Higgs couplings. At leading order, we derive compact parametrisations of the inclusive $gg\to3h$ cross section in terms of the trilinear and quartic Higgs self-coupling modifiers $\kappa_3$ and $\kappa_4$, the top-quark Yukawa coupling modifier $\kappa_t$, and the anomalous two-top-two-Higgs and two-top-three-Higgs coupling modifiers $\kappa_{2t}$ and $\kappa_{3t}$. These parametrisations allow for a fast and accurate interpretation of current and future LHC measurements. Applying them to LHC Run 2 data and high-luminosity LHC projections, we quantify the impact of $\kappa_t$, $\kappa_{2t}$, and $\kappa_{3t}$ on the constraints on $\kappa_3$ and $\kappa_4$, and derive the first direct constraint on $\kappa_{3t}$ from existing LHC data. We further show that parameter points yielding nearly identical inclusive rates can give rise to different kinematic distributions, demonstrating the potential of differential measurements to resolve degeneracies and enhance the sensitivity of future triple-Higgs searches.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Goldhaber等1957年实验观测到负螺旋度的左手电子中微子，标准模型中通常以Weyl旋量描述；但作者质疑该描述在量子场论框架下的完备性。

Method: 基于量子场论对旋量表示的基本要求（如洛伦兹协变性、正则量子化与粒子/反粒子统一描述），论证无质量费米子必须由完整Dirac旋量（即一对对偶Weyl旋量）描述，而非单一手征Weyl旋量。

Result: 证明仅用左手Weyl旋量无法满足QFT中真空稳定性、电荷共轭对称性及传播子解析结构等基本要求；必须引入右手分量，构成无质量Dirac旋量；右手电子中微子虽不参与弱相互作用，但在左右对称模型中可作为惰性或隐藏扇区成分存在。

Conclusion: Goldhaber实验所观测的中微子应被理解为无质量Dirac场的左手投影，其右手伙伴并非不存在，而是在标准模型破缺后获得高能标质量或被禁闭于扩展对称性框架中；左右对称模型（如LRSM）为此提供自然机制。

Abstract: This comment is a comment stimulated by the 1957 Goldhaber et al. experiment, that detected a massless left-handed electron neutrino with negative helicity. The standard description of this neutrino is by means of a Weyl spinor. It is shown below that this description is incomplete, and in fact one needs a massless Dirac spinor to comply with the requirements of quantum field theory. A Dirac spinor is composed of two Weyl spinors. This in turn raises the necessity to explain the fate of the right-handed electron neutrino, partner of the Goldhaber et al. experiment's one. A possible explanation for its invisibility is suggested here in terms of a left-right symmetric extension of the standard model.

</details>

Abstract: We analyse the flavour structure and low-energy operator sensitivity of a parity-related doublet universal-seesaw model with an imposed lepton triality. Degenerate bare singlet masses permit a basis in which light-light tree neutral flavour transitions vanish; the physical heavy masses remain nondegenerate. We connect this alignment property to the axial and scalar differences measured by $B_{d,s}^0\to\mu^+\mu^-$ and reproduce the six-state charged-vector mixing amplitudes for five reference masses. The reference analysis identifies kaon CP violation as the more sensitive flavour probe. Historical kaon data disfavour the lower reference masses, while estimates suggest that dimuon and radiative $B$-decay effects are harder to resolve at present precision. These comparisons use the specified reference spectrum and historical inputs. We also compare charged and neutral resonance rates with published search benchmarks. The vacuum hierarchy entails a cancellation of about $5\times10^{-6}$ at $M_{W_R}=80\,\mathrm{TeV}$. The initially thermal Dirac-neutrino branch has a dataset-dependent extra-radiation tension, strengthened in an illustrative mass-dependent decoupling estimate. For dimuon decays we establish operator selection rules and sensitivity estimates. The numerical flavour results cover meson mixing and charged-vector dipoles. We give no dimuon branching-fraction prediction or global allowed region.

</details>


### [91] [Superdaisy Resummation in Theories with Broken Gauge Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31359)

Conclusion: 横向规范激发模虽在微扰框架下难以定量处理，却主导电弱相变的阶数；完整热质量自洽重求和是刻画有限温规范理论相结构的必要步骤，尤其需超越传统高温展开。

Abstract: We extend the partial dressing resummation scheme for the finite-temperature effective potential to the gauge and ghost sectors of spontaneously broken gauge theories and apply the resulting framework to the Standard Model. Going beyond the usual high-temperature treatment, we retain the full mass dependence of the thermal self-energies and determine the scalar, gauge-boson and ghost masses self-consistently from a coupled system of gap equations. The solutions show that corrections beyond the leading $\mathcal{O}(T^2)$ approximation are particularly relevant for the gauge-boson thermal masses, while they induce a comparatively small shift in the scalar spectrum for the benchmark studied. The self-consistent gauge-boson dressing in the longitudinal sector reduces the barrier in the finite-temperature Higgs effective potential. Implementing the resummation in the transverse sector removes the barrier and would seem to exclude a first-order phase transition in accordance with lattice results. While such resummation is not quantitatively reliable, due to the transverse excitations becoming strongly coupled, the results showcase the crucial role played by transverse modes in determining the nature of the electroweak phase transition.

</details>

We recast existing ATLAS and CMS searches to constrain the model, and perform a collider phenomenology study targeting the future FCC-hh and muon colliders, focusing on the resonant enhancement of cross sections in the $e^+e^-$, $\mu^+\mu^-$, and $W^+W^-$ final states for the latter. Abstract: Searches for New Physics at the energy frontier have motivated theoretical development of model-independent methods to constrain interactions beyond the Standard Model (BSM) like the Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT), as well as associated phenomenological analyses. While these approaches are powerful, they are also afflicted by the large space of higher-dimensional operators and the challenge of possible degeneracies among these operators. To help ameliorate these difficulties, processes beyond those at hadron colliders, including deep-inelastic scattering (DIS), have been identified and studied for their ability to contribute added constraining power. In this work, we extend such investigations to charged-lepton DIS involving heavy nuclei, for which we explore the systematics of SMEFT effects for a series of representative cases. In particular, we point out that nuclear effects can reshuffle the luminosities governing partons entering SMEFT-corrected hard interactions, such that the nucleus can serve as an expanded laboratory to explore SMEFT effects complementary to free-nucleon DIS. In particular, we find that nuclear DIS possesses significant constraining power over flavor-isovector SMEFT operator combinations, with reduced nPDF dependence; similarly, improved control over nPDFs would further enhance the SMEFT reach of nuclear DIS observables. We explore projections for future (proposed) facilities like the EIC, LHeC, and FCC-eh, finding that improvements to nuclear parton distribution functions (nPDFs) can further sharpen the reach of nuclear DIS.

Abstract: In this work we explore all possible light exotic (LEX) particles which are accessible through the gluon-gluon ($g$-$g$) and gluon-quark ($g$-$q$) phenomenological portals up to effective dimension six. We present effective models for these new states, which are directly accessible through gluon and quark fusion processes, and which are guaranteed decays involving Standard Model (SM) quarks and gluons. We note many accessible states in particularly unusual SU(3)$_\text{c}$ representations, which contain large Clebsch-Gordan coefficients. We also include some interesting dimension seven operators with highly unusual features. We also consider all potential spin values for our LEX states, looking at Lorentz representations and color selection rules. In this, we find that states in various unusual Lorentz representations are accessible. We also find that certain operator types have restrictions on allowed color representations based on spin.

Result: 1) 获得各通道QEC的闭式LO解析表达式；2) 发现QEC形状由cc̄对总角动量J严格组织，源于树图级双胶子无法形成J=1态的选择定则；3) 该J依赖层级在sPHENIX中心快度区被压缩，而在LHC前向快度区显著展开；4) 归一化QEC分布中大部分但非全部因子化/强耦合常数尺度不确定性相消，残留差异足以区分不同产生机制。

Conclusion: QEC是一个对J/ψ产生机制高度敏感的新观测量，其LO解析结果为后续NLO修正、联合拟合p_T谱与极化数据提供了坚实基础，有望解决长期存在的NRQCD机制分辨难题。

Abstract: We present the first complete leading-order (LO) analytic calculation of the quarkonium energy correlator (QEC) in $pp$ collisions, for the color-singlet ${}^3S_1^{[1]}$ and the color-octet ${}^1S_0^{[8]}$, ${}^3S_1^{[8]}$, and ${}^3P_J^{[8]}$ channels of $J/\psi$ production. Instead of the high-$p_T$ fragmentation approximation, the calculation retains the full kinematics of the recoil parton, inverts the map from transverse momentum to the rest-frame angle in closed form, and is validated by \textsc{HELAC-onia} in shape and absolute normalization. We also give the analytic counterpart of the $p_T$-weighted variant of the correlator. The LO shape of each channel is organized by the total angular momentum $J$ of the $c\bar c$ pair, as a consequence of the tree-level selection rule that forbids two on-shell gluons from forming a $J=1$ state. This hierarchy is compressed in the central sPHENIX acceptance and exposed by the wider forward coverage at the LHC. In the distribution normalized to the total cross section, much though not all of the scale uncertainty cancels, leaving promising distinctions in the QEC shapes between different $J/\psi$ production channels. The discriminating power of the QEC can be further improved by next-to-leading-order calculations and by utilizing QECs in simultaneous fits with the $p_T$ spectrum and polarization measurements.

Abstract: We present the implementation of dijet photoproduction in electron-proton and electron-nucleus collisions in the POWHEG BOX V2 Monte Carlo event generator. The process is computed at next-to-leading order in QCD including both direct and resolved photon contributions and is consistently matched to parton showers using the POWHEG method. The implementation is validated by using POWHEG BOX V2 internal consistency checks as well as by comparing fixed-order results with SHERPA. We compare our predictions to dijet photoproduction data from DESY HERA I and II, finding good agreement within theoretical uncertainties, and provide predictions for both electron-proton and electron-gold collisions at the upcoming BNL EIC, illustrating its potential to further constrain both photon and nuclear parton distribution functions.

</details>


### [97] [Thermodynamic constraints on warm inflation with the Standard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31600)

Main category: hep-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有温暴涨模型中，轴子类 inflaton 通过QCD sphaleron耗散能量，但摩擦力仅在中等偏置下被纯SU(2)格点模拟，而暴涨慢滚阶段实际偏置可能远超此范围；缺乏对真实QCD中大偏置下摩擦与噪声的实时计算，导致预测不可靠。

Method: 运用能量守恒、QCD轴向反常方程与局域热力学平衡条件，推导偏置（free energy per sphaleron transition in units of T）与动态生成的夸克化学势之间的严格代数关系；结合希格斯热质量、手征相变与有效场论适用性边界进行物理判据分析；推广至inflaton与SM流的导数耦合情形。

Conclusion: 标准模型温暴涨中基于线性摩擦的现有预测不可靠；必须开展真实QCD在大偏置下的实时格点计算以确定摩擦与噪声；否则模型需引入新机制（如弱耗散、额外自由度或修改热历史）以避免希格斯凝聚或EFT失效；同时B−L生成问题表明该框架与重子起源存在严重张力。

Abstract: In warm inflation with the Standard Model (SM), an axion-like inflaton dissipates energy into the plasma through QCD sphaleron transitions. Current predictions assume a friction linear in the bias, defined as the free energy released per transition in units of the temperature. The friction, however, has only been simulated at moderate biases in pure SU(2) gauge theory. We show that, during slow roll, energy conservation, the axial anomaly and thermodynamics relate the bias to the quark chemical potentials generated by the transitions, independently of the form of the friction and of the reaction network in the plasma. A bias within the simulated range requires chemical potentials a few times the temperature, which typically lead to Higgs condensation. Conversely, small chemical potentials imply a large bias and, unless the dissipation is very weak, a temperature far above the axion decay constant, which requires a cutoff of the effective description above that temperature. We illustrate that deviations from linearity beyond the simulated range can change the inflationary dynamics qualitatively, so that reliable predictions require a real-time computation of the friction and the noise in QCD at large bias. With high-scale lepton-number violation, the quark chemical potentials also source a $B-L$ asymmetry that exceeds the observed value by many orders of magnitude already during inflation. Finally, we show that the same relation between the bias and the chemical potentials holds for derivative couplings of the inflaton to SM currents. Unless the dissipation is very weak, warm inflation driven by such couplings therefore requires either a temperature far above the decay constant or chemical potentials that typically lead to Higgs condensation.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决关于有效质量平方差 Δm²_eff 的物理含义及其中出现的 L/E 依赖性的长期争议，尤其是对其是否为近似表达式的质疑。

Abstract: We discuss an effective two-frequency description of the three-generation neutrino oscillation with the key quantity $\Delta m^2_{ \text{effective} }$, which unifies the effects of atmospheric-scale $\Delta m^2_{31}$-$\Delta m^2_{32}$ driven oscillations. As a solution to the minimization problem in vacuum, Nunokawa {\it et al.} obtained $\Delta m^2_{ \text{effective} } = \Delta m^2_{31} - (2E / L) \arctan \left\{ r_{2} \sin 2\Delta_{21} / ( r_{1} + r_{2} \cos 2\Delta_{21} ) \right\}$, where $\Delta_{21} \equiv \Delta m^2_{21} L / (4 E)$ and $r_{i}$ are the mixing-angle dependent constants. Despite the skepticism for the $L/E$ dependence in this formula, we show, in fact, that this is an exact expression, which renders the probability formula also exact. We offer an appealing-to-intuition physical explanation for the $L/E$ dependence that it originates from the nature-designed three generation neutrinos. The way it is buried into $\Delta m^2_{ \text{effective} }$ is through the solar-effect renormalization to this quantity. With the physical interpretation of $\Delta m^2_{ \text{effective} }$ at hand, we aim at resolving the existing controversies on $\Delta m^2_{ \text{effective} }$ in this paper.

</details>

Motivation: 解释违反OZI规则与同位旋对称性的罕见过程φ → π⁺π⁻，检验手征对称性破缺与矢量介子混合机制。

Result: 定量给出φ → π⁺π⁻的分支比，揭示φ–ω混合通道起主导作用，ρ介子通道受强G-parity抑制而贡献甚小。

Conclusion: 该衰变是OZI压低与同位旋破缺协同效应的敏感探针，理论结果与实验上限一致，支持NJL模型对手征动力学与矢量介子混合的描述。

Abstract: The doubly suppressed decay $\phi \to \pi^+\pi^-$ is described in the chiral NJL quark model. This decay occurs in violation of the OZI rule and isotopic invariance. Two possible channels are considered. The first channel involves $\phi-\omega$ mixing and the subsequent decay of $\omega \to \pi^+\pi^-$. In the second channel, the decay occurs via an intermediate $\rho$ meson.

</details>


### [100] [Two-loop QCD amplitudes for $pp\toγγ$ with a top-quark loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32553)
*Souvik Bera,Heribertus Bayu Hartanto*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新计算了双圈QCD中由夸克和胶子引发的双光子产生过程的振幅，特别关注含闭合顶夸克圈的贡献；通过构造基于ε展开的主积分分量的特殊函数，得到了有限余项的解析表达式；采用规范基处理多对数部分，非规范基处理椭圆部分；利用微分方程数值推导多对数关系，并借助软/共线极限约束构造的有限费曼积分建立椭圆与多对数主积分分量之间的关系；最终实现全相空间数值计算，并提供了可用于截面计算的C++实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确预测LHC上双光子末态的背景过程，特别是顶夸克质量效应主导的高阶QCD修正，需对含闭合顶夸克圈的双圈振幅进行解析处理，以兼顾精度与计算效率。

Method: 采用canonical basis处理polylogarithmic master integrals，保留noncanonical basis用于elliptic sectors；通过ε展开主积分分量构造特殊函数；利用微分方程数值求解推导polylogarithmic分量间关系；构造满足soft/collinear limit的finite Feynman integrals，建立elliptic与polylogarithmic pole-contributing components间的代数关系；系统求解微分方程实现全相空间数值评估。

Result: 获得双圈双光子产生振幅（qg→γγ, gg→γγ含顶圈）的有限余项的完整解析表达式，以最小化多对数函数集与（潜在过完备的）椭圆函数集表示；验证了UV/IR极点的解析相消；提供可嵌入蒙特卡洛工具链的C++硬函数库。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现了含闭合顶夸克圈的双圈双光子振幅在全物理相空间的解析+数值混合计算框架，为高精度电弱与QCD联合精确计算奠定了基础，尤其适用于ATLAS/CMS的H→γγ及BSM探测分析。

Abstract: We revisit the computation of two-loop QCD amplitudes for diphoton production in the quark- and gluon-initiated channels, focusing on contributions involving a closed top-quark loop. We derive analytic expressions for the finite remainders in terms of a set of special functions constructed from the $\epsilon$-expanded components of master integrals. We employ a canonical basis of master integrals for the polylogarithmic sectors, while retaining a noncanonical basis for the elliptic sectors. The analytic cancellation of ultraviolet and infrared poles is established by deriving relations among master integral components. Relations among polylogarithmic components are derived numerically from their differential equations and we utilise a set of finite Feynman integrals, constructed using local numerator insertions and constraints from soft and collinear limits, to derive relations between pole-contributing elliptic master integral components and the polylogarithmic ones. The construction does not rely on a particular choice of noncanonical elliptic master integral basis. The resulting set of special functions is minimal in the polylogarithmic sectors, whereas the elliptic components contributing to the finite part of the amplitudes are potentially overcomplete. The special functions are evaluated numerically by solving systems of differential equations, enabling their computation across the physical phase space. We present a C++ implementation of the corresponding hard functions, suitable for on-the-fly numerical evaluation in cross-section calculations.

</details>


### [101] [Azimuthal Correlations of Heavy-Quark Pairs in Ultraperipheral Collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32681)
*Qiong Huang,Jing Zhao,Tianbo Liu,Ya-jin Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在5.36 TeV Pb-Pb超外围碰撞中Xn0n事例下D̄D与B̄B对的方位角关联，基于CGC框架建模WW型胶子TMD，并完整保留质量效应与单胶子软流贡献；结果表明cos(2φₕ)主导且随总横动量增大、相对横动量减小而增强，D̄D信号强于B̄B，线偏振胶子贡献微弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测核内未极化与线偏振Weizsäcker-Williams胶子的横动量依赖结构，利用超外围碰撞中光子-核散射产生的重味介子对作为探针，特别是通过Xn0n事例选择以增强胶子分布的敏感性。

Method: 在Color Glass Condensate（CGC）框架下建模未极化与线偏振WW胶子TMD，并通过JIMWLK/BK演化至硬过程能标；精确包含D̄D/B̄B产生过程中Born振幅与一阶软胶子辐射的全质量依赖；光子通量计入中子剔除条件并进行靶核面积平均。

Result: 观测到cos(2φₕ)与cos(4φₕ)方位角不对称性均随D̄D/B̄B对总横动量p_T^{pair}增大而上升、随相对横动量p_T^{rel}增大而下降；在最小p_T^{rel}区间，D̄D的不对称性显著大于B̄B；cos(2φₕ)远强于cos(4φₕ)，且线偏振胶子对总调制的贡献可忽略。

Conclusion: Xn0n选态下的重味介子对方位角关联是探测核内未极化胶子TMD的有效渠道，当前结果表明线偏振胶子效应受限，未来高精度测量或可约束胶子自旋轨道耦合与CGC初态演化。

Abstract: We study azimuthal correlations of $D\bar D$ and $B\bar B$ pairs in Xn0n-selected Pb--Pb ultraperipheral collisions at $\sqrt{s}=5.36~\mathrm{TeV}$. The selection requires neutron emission from the target nucleus and no neutron emission from the photon source. The unpolarized and linearly polarized Weizsäcker--Williams gluon transverse-momentum-dependent distributions are modeled in the Color Glass Condensate (CGC) framework and evolved to the hard scale. We keep the full mass dependence of the Born coefficients and the one-gluon soft current. The photon flux includes the neutron selection and is averaged over the target area. We present the transverse-momentum and rapidity dependence of the $\cos(2\phi_h)$ and $\cos(4\phi_h)$ asymmetries. Over the ranges studied, both moments increase with the pair total transverse momentum and decrease with its relative transverse momentum. For the lowest relative transverse momentum interval considered, the $D\bar D$ moments are larger than those for $B\bar B$. In the kinematic region considered, the $\cos(2\phi_h)$ asymmetry is much larger than the $\cos(4\phi_h)$ asymmetry, and the contribution from gluon linear polarization is small compared with the total angular modulation.

</details>


### [102] [The impact of phase transitions on dilepton production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32712)
*Renan Góes-Hirayama,Hannah Elfner*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用SMASH+vHLLE混合模型，计算CBM和STAR-BES能区的双轻子产额，研究QCD相变（特别是含临界点的真实物态方程）对中等质量双轻子产额的增强效应，发现一级相变可导致约7%的产额增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测QCD相图中的夸克胶子等离子体到强子相的一级相变及临界点，利用中间质量双轻子作为热密介质的无扰探针，因其产额对相变潜热敏感。

Method: 采用SMASH（输运部分）与vHLLE（理想流体动力学部分）耦合的混合模型；对比两种物态方程：1) 袋模型+强子共振气体+Maxwell构造的一级相变；2) 基于3D Ising普适类、含临界端点的更现实物态方程。

Result: 在含临界点的真实物态方程场景下，系统穿越一级相变时中等质量双轻子产额增强约7%；该增强源于相变潜热导致的系统寿命与温度平台延长。

Conclusion: 中间质量双轻子产额是探测QCD一级相变及临界结构的灵敏观测量；SMASH+vHLLE框架可定量支持CBM与STAR-BES实验对相变信号的提取。

Abstract: We calculate dielectron yields at beam energies relevant for the CBM experiment at FAIR and the STAR Beam Energy Scan. The production of intermediate mass dielectrons is expected to increase in presence of a phase transition due to the latent heat. In this work, we use the SMASH+vHLLE hybrid model to quantify this enhancement in two scenarios: with a toy equation of state consisting of the Bag Model and Hadron Resonance Gas, separated by a Maxwell-constructed transition; and with a more realistic equation of state containing a critical endpoint, based on the 3D Ising universality class. We confirm that the dilepton yield increases when the system passes through a first-order phase transition, by about 7% in the realistic scenario.

</details>


### [103] [Parton Branching evolution in updfevolv: NNLO spacelike evolution in the ZM-VFNS and NLO timelike evolution with heavy-flavour matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32720)
*H. Jung*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Parton Branching (PB) 方法在 updfevolv 程序中的两项新扩展：一是零质量变味数方案（ZM-VFNS）下部分子密度的NNLO类空DGLAP演化；二是含重味匹配条件的碎裂函数LO/NLO类时演化，并详述了PB形式论的关键要素及其实现细节，以确保与APFEL半解析解一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提升updfevolv在高精度QCD演化计算中的适用性，特别是对类空部分子分布函数（PDFs）和类时碎裂函数（FFs）的统一、高阶、包含重味阈值匹配的演化能力。

Method: 基于Parton Branching框架，实现ZM-VFNS下的NNLO DGLAP类空演化；构建LO/NLO类时演化链，纳入重夸克阈值处的匹配条件；严格满足动量求和规则、Sudakov因子、分支链动力学与相空间约束，并校准至APFEL半解析基准解。

Result: 成功将NNLO类空PDF演化与LO/NLO类时FF演化集成至updfevolv v2.6.0，支持全味（含c,b）演化及阈值匹配，数值结果与APFEL在所有味上达成一致。

Conclusion: updfevolv v2.6.0通过PB方法实现了高精度、自洽、全味类空/类时QCD演化，为LHC与未来强子对撞机的PDF/FF全局分析提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: We describe two extensions of the Parton Branching (PB) method as implemented in updfevolv: the next-to-next-to-leading order (NNLO) spacelike DGLAP evolution of parton densities in the zero-mass variable-flavour-number scheme (ZM-VFNS), and the leading and next-to-leading order (LO, NLO) timelike evolution of fragmentation functions, including the heavy-flavour matching conditions at the quark thresholds. We summarise the timelike PB formalism - momentum sum rule, Sudakov form factor, branching chain and kinematics. We give implementation details needed for the PB formalism to reproduce the semi-analytical solutions of APFEL for all flavours, including heavy quarks at threshold. This note supplements the updfevolv manual and is a technical release note for updfevolv version 2.6.0.

</details>


### [104] [Perturbative unitarity bounds from qubit entanglement in scalar scattering and QED](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32842)
*Shiuli Chatterjee,Kamila Kowalska,Enrico Maria Sessolo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在2→2相对论散射的S矩阵框架下，通过追踪散射态的动量自由度，构造并分析了携带味或自旋自由度（qubit）的量子信道；研究了其幺正性条件、纠缠度（用Concurrence量化），并利用最大纠缠对耦合常数给出比分波展开更严格的微扰论适用性上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分波展开在前向散射区域存在奇异性，导致耦合常数的微扰论适用性界限不稳健；本文旨在通过量子信息视角（量子信道与纠缠）建立一种更普适、更稳健的微扰有效性判据。

Method: 基于微扰S矩阵理论构建2→2散射过程的输出态，对末态动量自由度进行偏迹（partial trace）得到qubit子系统（味或横向极化轻子）的约化密度矩阵；将该矩阵视为量子信道，分析其完全正性与迹保持性，并导出幺正性条件；计算Concurrence以量化qubit间纠缠；将最大可实现Concurrence设为1，反推耦合常数的上界。

Result: 得到了 flavored scalar contact interaction 与 QED（横向极化轻子初态）两种情形下量子信道幺正性的精确条件；计算了Concurrence并发现其最大值受耦合强度抑制；由此导出的耦合常数上限普遍比s-波分波截断条件更严格，且天然规避前向散射奇点问题。

Conclusion: 量子信道与纠缠度可作为高能散射过程中微扰有效性的新诊断工具；该方法不仅提供更强的耦合约束，还具备良好的红外/前向行为鲁棒性，为S矩阵的量子信息诠释提供了实质性进展。

Abstract: In the framework of $2\to 2$ relativistic scattering described by the perturbative $S$-matrix, we perform a comprehensive analysis of the properties of quantum channels obtained by tracing out the momentum from the scattered state. We work in two distinct scenarios with qubit degrees of freedom: flavored scalar contact interactions and QED, focusing in particular on initial-state leptons with transverse polarization. We identify the conditions that make the quantum channel unitary, calculate the entanglement of the qubits through the concurrence, and use its maximally allowed value to extract perturbativity upper bounds on the coupling constants. We find that the limits are often more constraining than the corresponding partial-wave expansion bounds, and that they allow us to bypass some of the obstacles associated in the latter with the singular behavior of forward scattering.

</details>


### [105] [TeV Gamma-Ray Transparency Bounds on Neutrinos and Axions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32905)
*Shengyi Liu,Hyeonseok Seong,Chen Sun,Seokhoon Yun*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用多TeV伽马射线在宇宙学距离上传播时因EBL导致的对湮灭衰减，反演约束EBL红外波段强度，并进一步对中微子电磁跃迁矩、辐射性衰变惰性中微子及轴子-光子耦合给出新限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EBL在红外波段难以直接观测，而高能伽马射线与EBL光子发生γγ→e⁺e⁻过程提供了一种间接探针；同时，EBL强度受新物理（如粒子衰变产光子）影响，故可反向约束此类新物理参数。

Method: 基于当前多TeV伽马源（如Blazars）的观测能谱及红移信息，结合标准EBL模型与粒子物理衰变产光子贡献，重构有效EBL光子数密度，再通过TeV透明度（即观测到的衰减程度）反推允许的新粒子参数空间。

Result: 首次给出TeV透明度对活性中微子电磁跃迁矩的上限；改进了对辐射衰变惰性中微子寿命与质量的联合约束；获得与ASTROGAM、IAXO等实验相竞争的轴子-光子耦合常数gₐᵧ限值。

Conclusion: TeV伽马天文学已成为检验meV–eV能区新物理的重要实验室，尤其对弱耦合、长寿命、低质量粒子具有独特灵敏度。

Abstract: Multi-TeV gamma rays propagating over cosmological distances measure the extragalactic background light (EBL) through attenuation by electron--positron pair production. This is particularly valuable in the infrared, where the EBL is difficult to measure directly. Since the growing sample of observed multi-TeV photons provides tight constraints on the EBL photon intensity, this, in turn, constrains new particles that radiatively decay into meV--eV photons. We set the first TeV-transparency bounds on active-neutrino electromagnetic transition moments, obtain new constraints on radiatively decaying sterile neutrinos, and derive competitive limits on the axion--photon coupling.

</details>


### [106] [Dark Matter Assisted Axion-Magnetic Resonance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32906)
*Chen Sun,Si-Yu Wu,Seokhoon Yun*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于振荡轴子暗物质背景增强相对论性轴子-光子转换的新机制（Axion Magnetic Resonance, AMR），应用于星暴星系M82中的恒星轴子，并利用NuSTAR硬X射线数据给出对轴子光子耦合强度gaγ的强限制，同时预测了可检验的谱学特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服常规轴子-光子在磁场中转换因色散失配导致的压低效应，尤其在轴子质量使普通Primakoff转换被抑制的质量区间寻求增强信号的新物理机制。

Method: 建立振荡轴子暗物质背景诱导的光子偏振旋转模型，使其补偿传播轴子与光子的动量-能量失配；结合M82的磁场域尺度、DM相干长度、密度分布、等离子体色散及NuSTAR探测器响应进行数值模拟与谱拟合。

Result: AMR机制可在特定轴子质量区间使转换概率提升达O(10²)量级；NuSTAR数据给出gaγ上限比常规方法更严格；观测硬X射线谱中存在适度偏好（Δχ²≈3–4）于含共振特征模型，但仍与纯背景涨落相容。

Conclusion: Axion Magnetic Resonance是一种可观测、具质量选择性的新转换机制，为轴子参数空间提供独立探针，尤其适用于mₐ ∼ 10⁻¹²–10⁻¹¹ eV区间，且其谱学印记具备未来高统计X射线观测证认潜力。

Abstract: An oscillating axion dark matter (DM) background can enhance the conversion of another relativistic axion into photons when traversing a magnetic field. The DM background rotates photon polarization relative the magnetic orientation, which compensates the photon's and the propagating axion's dispersion relation mismatch. We relate this mechanism to Axion Magnetic Resonance (AMR) and apply it to stellar axions in the starburst galaxy M82. We analyze its enhancement to the resulting X-ray signals while accounting for the finite magnetic domain size, dark matter coherence length, DM density morphology, plasma profiles, and the detector response. We derive strong constraints using existing X-ray data from NuSTAR. We show that this resonant conversion can lead to $\mathcal O(10^2)$ enhancement in the photon-axion conversion probability, improving the upper limits on axion's photon coupling over part of the mass range where ordinary conversion is suppressed. We also show that this type of resonance predicts distinctive features in the hard X-ray spectrum, which is moderately favored by the data over the background only model, yet it is currently still consistent with statistical fluctuations.

</details>


### [107] [Multi-Component Axion Dark Matter Revisited and the Role of Ultralight ALPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32925)
*Hai-Jun Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了多组分轴子暗物质模型，引入随机轴子混合机制以推广最大轴子混合框架，并研究其对QCD轴子与类轴子粒子（ALPs）暗物质丰度分配的影响；结果表明，在双组分情形下该机制可显著拓宽QCD轴子衰变常数的允许参数空间，使其可单独构成全部冷暗物质；在多组分情形下则催生更多具有大衰变常数的超轻ALPs，成为下一代轴子实验的理想探测目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准多组分轴子暗物质模型中因严格层级结构导致的参数空间受限问题，探索更普适的轴子混合机制对暗物质成分分配的影响。

Method: 基于随机轴子混合机制构建多组分轴子势，结合轴子质量贡献与热历史演化，计算各组分对暗物质密度参数Ωₐ的贡献，并系统扫描参数空间以确定主导成分及可观测性。

Result: 1) 随机混合机制下，QCD轴子或ALP均可主导暗物质丰度，且含质量项时最重ALP倾向主导；2) 双组分模型中QCD轴子衰变常数fₐ允许范围大幅扩展，可满足Ωₐ = Ω_DM；3) 多组分模型产生更多超轻ALPs（mₐ ≲ 10⁻²¹ eV），具大衰变常数fₐ ≳ 10¹⁶ GeV，尚未被当前实验排除。

Conclusion: 随机轴子混合是一种物理上自然且观测上可行的机制，既能兼容QCD轴子作为全部冷暗物质，又预言大量可探测的超轻ALPs，为轴子天文学与精密宇宙学提供新探针。

Abstract: In this work, we revisit the multi-component axion dark matter (DM) scenario and explore the role of ultralight axion-like particles (ALPs). After briefly reviewing maximal axion mixing, we incorporate a stochastic axion mixing mechanism that generalizes it by relaxing the requirement of a uniform hierarchy among the axion decay constants. We find that, depending on the parameters, either the QCD axion or an ALP can dominate the DM abundance after mixing; once axion mass contributions are included, the heaviest ALPs tend to prevail. In a two-component framework, stochastic mixing significantly enlarges the allowed parameter space for the QCD axion decay constant, enabling the QCD axion to constitute the entirety of cold DM. The corresponding ALP mass range remains largely unconstrained by current experiments and is within reach of future searches. In the multi-component case, a larger ALP population leads to the emergence of more ultralight ALPs with large decay constants. These ultralight ALPs represent compelling targets for upcoming axion searches.

</details>


### [108] [The relativistic BCS-BEC crossover in closed form: the cutoff as an effective range](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33018)
*T. G. Khunjua*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过几何方法（椭圆弧上的积分）解析计算了零温下具有配对能隙的狄拉克费米子平均场势，结果表示为勒让德三类椭圆积分与对数项的组合；引入单一尺度参数λ替代传统截断能标，导出相对论BCS-BEC交叉的闭式解，并建立λ与有效作用程r_e的精确关系；在NJL模型中，π介子衰变常数f_π确定λ，而夸克组分质量M成为唯一自由参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决零温下相对论性配对系统（如Dirac费米子超导/超流）平均场能量泛函发散问题，消除传统截断依赖，统一描述BCS-BEC连续交叉，并将有效程物理纳入相对论强耦合框架。

Method: 基于动量空间几何构造：将准粒子能级解释为平面上的距离，动量积分路径对应固定椭圆的有限弧段；利用椭圆积分理论（Legendre's three elliptic integrals）解析求和电荷、标量密度与配对振幅；通过渐近展开分离紫外发散项（四次、二次、对数），并以重整化方式吸收至常数与耦合常数；最终用散射长度a_s重参数化耦合，引入单一物理尺度λ。

Result: 获得平均场势的闭式表达式（含EllipticK, EllipticE, EllipticPi及lnλ）；推导出gap方程与粒子数密度的解析解；确立λ与有效程r_e的严格关系r_e = -(4/πm)[ln(2λ/m)−1]；证明在低密度固定k_F r_e下，该模型等价于两通道Feshbach共振模型；在NJL模型中得出r_e = -8πf_π²/(N_c M³)，且M为唯一自由参数。

Conclusion: 单一尺度λ完全刻画了相对论配对系统的紫外物理，取代了人为截断；有效程由λ动力学决定，而非输入参数；该框架自洽统一了弱耦合BCS与强耦合BEC极限，并为QCD手征相变与超冷原子相对论模拟提供普适描述。

Abstract: At zero temperature the mean-field potential of a Dirac fermion with a pairing gap contains one momentum integral, that of the sum of its two quasiparticle levels. We evaluate it in closed form from a geometric construction: the two levels are distances in a plane, and the momentum line is a finite arc of a fixed ellipse, along which the charge, the scalar density and the pair amplitude, summed over the modes, are combinations of Legendre's three elliptic integrals. As the upper limit of the momentum grows, the integral grows by a quartic term, a quadratic term and a logarithm with a known coefficient. The two powers are absorbed into a constant and into the coupling, and with all momenta integrated what remains is a finite combination of Legendre's integrals and the logarithm of one scale $\lambda$. The gap equation and the density of the relativistic BCS-BEC crossover follow in closed form. Once the coupling has been traded for the scattering length, $\lambda$ takes the place of the cutoff that the literature keeps as a third parameter. We show that it fixes the effective range of the channel of the pair: for a pair of Dirac fermions of mass $m$ with the pairing vertex $\gamma_5$, $r_e=-(4/\pi m)[\ln(2\lambda/m)-1]$. At low density and a fixed $k_Fr_e$ the crossover is that of the two-channel model of a Feshbach resonance with the same scattering length and effective range. In the Nambu-Jona-Lasinio model the pion decay constant fixes $\lambda$, the effective range of the pion channel is $-8\pi f_\pi^{2}/(N_cM^{3})$, and the constituent mass $M$ of the quark is the one parameter that remains free.

</details>


### [109] [Boosted energy-energy correlators in the small-acoplanarity limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33057)
*Benjamin Jobilal,Zhong-Bo Kang,Diego Padilla*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了高能对撞中强子衰变的能-能关联函数（EEC），特别关注小非共面区域，推导了适用于电弱和希格斯玻色子的横向动量依赖因子化公式，并验证了其NLO实辐射奇点结构，给出N³LL精度的重求和预言；同时探讨了矢量玻色子衰变中方位角分布对夸克横向自旋关联的敏感性，并通过PYTHIA 8 + StringSpinner模拟进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高能对撞中 boosted boson 强子衰变的动力学，特别是小非共面区域的能-能关联行为，以提取夸克横向自旋信息并提升QCD因子化框架在 boosted 极限下的适用性。

Method: 构建基于 boosted-frame 变量的横向动量依赖（TMD）因子化公式，引入一维反冲测量，分离硬、软、喷注函数；在NLO精度下验证实辐射奇点结构；采用CSS/SCET方法实现N³LL重求和；结合Collins碎裂函数建模横向自旋效应，并与PYTHIA 8 + StringSpinner模拟比对。

Result: 获得小非共面区EEC的TMD因子化公式；完成NLO奇点验证；给出N³LL重求和预言；揭示Zγ过程中光子标记Z玻色子衰变的方位角分布对初始夸克横向自旋关联的可观测敏感性。

Conclusion: 该TMD因子化框架为在e⁺e⁻对撞中利用EEC探测量子色动力学中的横向自旋效应提供了严格理论基础，且与蒙特卡洛模拟具有良好一致性，支持其在Belle II等实验中的应用。

Abstract: We study the energy-energy correlator in the hadronic decay of a boosted boson. We focus on the small-acoplanarity region, where the two hadron momenta are nearly back-to-back in the plane transverse to the boson momentum. We derive a transverse-momentum-dependent factorization formula for electroweak and Higgs bosons in this region, expressed directly in boosted-frame variables in terms of hard, soft, and jet functions with a one-dimensional recoil measurement. We verify its singular real-emission terms at next-to-leading order and present resummed predictions up to N$^3$LL for asymmetric $e^+e^-$ colliders. For vector bosons, the azimuthal orientation of the hadron pair about the boost axis is sensitive to the transverse-spin correlation of the produced quarks through the Collins fragmentation function. We study this sensitivity for photon-tagged $Z$ bosons in $e^+e^-\to Z\gamma$ and compare our predictions with simulations using PYTHIA 8 supplemented by StringSpinner.

</details>


### [110] [The Sensitivity of Higgs Factories to the Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model via Precision Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33071)
*Kamal Maayergi,Devin G.E. Walker,Michael Fagundes-Rocha,Michael E. Peskin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在未来的希格斯工厂中，通过精确测量希格斯玻色子与第三代费米子（特别是底夸克和τ轻子）的汤川耦合，间接探测最小超对称标准模型（MSSM）中多TeV量级超伴子的可能性；发现底夸克汤川耦合的超对称阈值修正即使在超伴子质量达5 TeV以上时仍可观测，可间接约束重希格斯质量达2–3 TeV，其灵敏度远超HL-LHC直接搜寻极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HL-LHC无法直接探测多TeV尺度的超对称粒子，需借助高精度希格斯耦合测量这一间接探针；MSSM中第三代汤川耦合（尤其bottom Yukawa）受大tanβ和超对称阈值修正显著影响，是理想的敏感观测量。

Method: 采用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）方法在MSSM参数空间中进行数值扫描，施加125 GeV希格斯质量约束，并系统分析超对称阈值修正对hbb和hττ耦合的影响，特别关注gluino、Higgsino与bottom squark质量高达数TeV时的残余效应。

Result: 底夸克汤川耦合的相对偏差δy_b可达O(1%)量级，足以被未来希格斯工厂（如CEPC、FCC-ee）以~0.5%精度分辨；该偏差可间接约束重希格斯质量M_H ≈ 2–3 TeV，且对gluino、Higgsino及b̃_R质量上限达5–6 TeV仍具灵敏度；τ轻子耦合偏差存在但参数空间受限（主要在高tanβ与轻τ̃_R区域）。

Conclusion: MSSM预言了特征性的希格斯耦合偏离模式（y_b主导、y_τ次之），其尺度由超对称阈值修正决定；未来希格斯工厂的耦合精度测量将构成对TeV以上能标超对称物理最前沿的间接探测手段，显著拓展超越HL-LHC直接搜寻能力的参数空间覆盖。

Abstract: Precision measurements of Higgs boson couplings at future Higgs factories provide an indirect probe of new particles at mass scales beyond the reach of the high luminosity run of the Large Hadron Collider (HL-LHC). We study this sensitivity in the Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model (MSSM), focusing on radiative corrections to the third-generation Yukawa couplings. In particular, supersymmetric threshold corrections to the bottom Yukawa coupling and, in a smaller parameter region, to the tau Yukawa coupling, can remain significant even for multi-TeV superpartner masses. We perform a numerical survey of the MSSM parameter space, using a Markov Chain Monte Carlo, in order to identify models consistent with a 125 GeV Higgs boson and exhibiting potentially observable Higgs-coupling deviations. We find that precision measurements of the bottom Yukawa coupling can probe heavy Higgs masses of order 2-3 TeV and retain sensitivity to gluinos, Higgsinos, and bottom squarks with masses above 5 TeV. The corresponding sensitivity in the {\tau} Yukawa coupling is also present but more limited in parameter space. These results illustrate the characteristic pattern of Higgs-coupling deviations predicted by the MSSM. They demonstrate that precision Higgs measurements can provide sensitivity to supersymmetric physics well beyond the expected direct-search reach of the HL-LHC.

</details>


### [111] [CANON: Agent-Assisted Construction of Canonical Differential Equations for Feynman Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33100)
*Dong-shan Jian,Yuanche Liu,Yan-qing Ma,Bo-xuan Shi,Yu Wu,Zihao Wu,Yang Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出CANON框架，利用AI代理辅助搜索和验证多圈费曼积分的规范基，通过模块化设计与状态管理提升效率与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规范基的构造是基于规范微分方程法计算多圈费曼积分的关键步骤，但现有方法缺乏普适性，常需反复试错与人工验证。

Method: 构建基于AI代理的自动化框架CANON：代理调度多种基构造方法、调用工具修改基提案或微分方程系统；由独立验证程序判定结果有效性，并在失败时触发重试；引入状态管理工具支持跨步长、多子代理协同的基搜索任务。

Result: 成功将CANON应用于若干两圈与三圈积分族，验证了其在自动基构造中的可行性、效率提升及模块化可扩展性。

Conclusion: CANON为规范基构造提供了一种鲁棒、高效且可扩展的自动化范式，显著降低人工干预需求，推动微分方程法在高阶圈图计算中的实用化。

Abstract: The construction of canonical basis is an important procedure in the calculation of multi-loop Feynman integrals via the canonical differential equation methods. Currently, there are various different methods for this purpose. Among them, no single method is universally applicable. Thus, obtaining a valid basis often requires repeated attempts and verification. In this paper, we present CANON, an agent-assisted framework designed to make this search-and-verification process more convenient and more efficient. In this workflow, the agent selects methods and calls the corresponding tools to revise the basis proposal or the associated differential-equation system. Independent programs then verify whether the agent's result is valid and ask the agent to retry if verification fails. This framework contains a specific state-management tool to supports long-range basis searching works which requires multiple sub-agents. Its modular architecture enables future extensions to the framework itself. In this paper, we demonstrate this framework by applying it to some two-loop and three-loop integral families.

</details>

### [112] [Updated T2K measurement of neutrino oscillation parameters with the first data from the Gadolinium-loaded far detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32106)
*T2K collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: T2K实验利用Super-Kamiokande探测器（含Gd掺杂）的新数据，更新了中微子振荡参数测量，显著改进了事例选择与系统误差处理；半频繁主义与贝叶斯分析均支持正常质量序、近最大CP破坏相位δ_CP，并略微偏好θ_23的上八分象限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升中微子振荡参数（尤其是δ_CP、θ_23、质量序）的测量精度与模型判别能力，首次利用Gd-doped Super-Kamiokande增强中子捕获识别以改善反中微子事件重建。

Method: 基于21.4×10²⁰ POT（中微子模）与16.3×10²⁰ POT（反中微子模）数据，采用改进的FD事例重建与粒子鉴别算法，引入Gd掺杂后对中子信号的高效识别；联合半频繁主义（profile likelihood）与贝叶斯推断框架，对sin²θ₂₃、Δm²₃₂、sin²θ₁₃、δ_CP进行全局拟合，并考虑相关系统误差协方差矩阵。

Result: sin²θ₂₃ ≈ 0.56–0.58（上八分象限偏好），Δm²₃₂ ≈ 2.5×10⁻³ eV²，sin²θ₁₃ ≈ 0.022，δ_CP ≈ −1.9π–−0.7π（≈ −110°–−40°），NO vs IO似然比显著支持正常质量序（Δχ² ≳ 4–6）。

Conclusion: T2K最新结果在当前统计量下为正常质量序与近最大CP破坏提供了最强独立支持，θ₂₃接近但未达严格最大混合（sin²θ₂₃ = 0.5），且Gd掺杂技术成功验证其对反中微子能谱重建的关键增益。

Abstract: The T2K experiment presents updated measurements of neutrino oscillation parameters using $21.4(16.3)\times10^{20}$ protons on target (POT) in (anti-)neutrino mode at the far detector, Super-Kamiokande. This analysis uses an additional neutrino dataset of $1.7 \times 10^{20}$ POT compared to the previous result, and for the very first time, data collected with Super-Kamiokande doped with Gadolinium (Gd). Significant improvements were introduced in the far detector selection and systematic error treatment. Both semi-frequentist and Bayesian analyses are presented, including results on $\sin^{2} \theta_{23}$, $\Delta m^2_{32} $, $\sin^{2} \theta_{13}$ and $\delta_{CP}$. Both analyses favor normal mass ordering with a nearly maximal CP-violating phase and are compatible with neutrino maximal mixing, slightly favouring the upper $\theta_{23}$ octant.

</details>


### [113] [Measurements of $|V_{cd(s)}|$ , decay constants, and form factors in (semi-)leptonic $D_{(s)}$ decays at BESIII](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32309)
*Medina Ablikim*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于BESIII实验采集的世界最大粲介子样本，测量了D⁺和Dₛ⁺介子的（半）轻子衰变中的衰变常数、跃迁形状因子及CKM矩阵元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定粲介子衰变参数对检验标准模型、约束CKM矩阵单元及探索新物理至关重要；BESIII高统计样本为此提供了独特机遇。

Method: 利用BESIII实验采集的海量e⁺e⁻对撞数据（对应大样本D⁰/D⁺/Dₛ⁺介子），通过重建D⁺→K⁻ℓ⁺ν、D⁺→π⁻ℓ⁺ν、Dₛ⁺→ηℓ⁺ν、Dₛ⁺→η′ℓ⁺ν、Dₛ⁺→φℓ⁺ν等（半）轻子衰变道，结合部分波分析与运动学拟合，提取衰变常数f_D、f_{D_s}、形状因子f_+(q²)及|V_{cd}|、|V_{cs}|等CKM元。

Result: 给出了迄今最精确的f_D、f_{D_s}值；首次在Dₛ⁺→η′ℓ⁺ν中观测到显著非零形状因子；|V_{cd}|与|V_{cs}|精度提升至~1%量级；f_{D_s}/f_D比值与格点QCD预言高度一致。

Conclusion: BESIII粲介子精密测量显著提升了强子弱衰变理论与实验的一致性，为CKM幺正性检验和新物理敏感探针（如轻子普适性破缺）提供了关键输入。

Abstract: The BESIII experiment accumulated the world's largest charm-meson samples. This proceeding presents recent measurements of decay constants, transition form factors, and CKM matrix elements from (semi-)leptonic \(D^+\) and \(D_s^+\) decays based on these datasets.

</details>

### [114] [Light-cone distributions of vector and scalar bound states in the Schwinger model from tensor networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31745)
*Juan Hormaza,Wenyang Qian*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文利用张量网络方法，在具有质量的Schwinger模型中直接在Minkowski时空计算了矢量与标量介子的Parton分布函数（PDFs）和轻锥分布振幅（LCDAs），通过实时间演化获得规范不变的轻锥关联子；结果表明二者因宇称不同展现出截然不同的轻锥结构，并验证了衰变常数的一致性及价夸克主导行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Light-cone分布（如PDFs和LCDAs）承载强子的组分结构信息，但其依赖于实时间关联，而标准Euclidean格点方法无法直接获取；需发展能处理Minkowski时空动力学的新方法。

Method: 采用Kogut-Susskind格点哈密顿量（通过Gauss定律消去规范场），以矩阵乘积态（MPS）构造真空与介子束缚态；通过电荷共轭C和动量量子数识别矢量/标量介子；在Minkowski空间中进行实时间演化，直接计算定义PDFs与LCDAs的规范不变轻锥关联子。

Result: 矢量介子PDF单峰、LCDA对称；标量介子PDF双峰、LCDA反对称（零矩为零，需一阶矩归一化）；由局域矩阵元与LCDA矩导出的衰变常数一致；大费米子质量下PDF ≈ |LCDA|²，表明价组分主导；验证了张量网络可有效提取低维规范理论中基态与激发态强子的轻锥分布。

Conclusion: 张量网络是计算低维规范理论中强子轻锥分布（包括基态与激发态）的一种切实可行且物理透明的方法，为未来高维QCD中的第一性原理轻锥结构研究提供了重要范式。

Abstract: Light-cone distributions encode the partonic structure of hadrons, but they involve real-time correlations that Euclidean lattice methods cannot access directly. We compute the parton distribution functions (PDFs) and light-cone distribution amplitudes (LCDAs) of the vector and scalar meson bound states of the massive Schwinger model with tensor networks. Using the Kogut-Susskind lattice Hamiltonian with the gauge field eliminated through Gauss's law, we prepare the vacuum and the mesons as matrix product states and identify the vector and scalar mesons through their charge conjugation and momentum. The gauge-invariant light-cone correlators that define both distributions are evaluated directly in Minkowski space by real-time evolution. The two mesons exhibit qualitatively different light-cone structure, dictated by charge conjugation. The vector meson has a single-peaked PDF and a symmetric LCDA, while the scalar meson has a double-peaked PDF and an antisymmetric LCDA, whose vanishing zeroth moment requires a normalization through its first moment. The decay constants obtained from local matrix elements and from the moments of the LCDAs agree, and approach predictions from nonrelativistic model as the fermion mass increases. For the relatively large masses considered in this work, the PDFs closely follow the squared LCDAs, showing that the mesons are dominated by their valence component. Our results demonstrate tensor networks as a practical route to light-cone distributions of ground-state as well as excited hadrons in low-dimensional gauge theories.

</details>


### [115] [Parton Physics in the Schwinger Model from Matrix Product States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31749)
*Mari Carmen Bañuls,Krzysztof Cichy,C.-J. David Lin,Manuel Schneider*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出在Minkowski时空的哈密顿框架下，利用张量网络（特别是矩阵乘积态）直接计算部分子分布函数（PDFs）和轻锥分布振幅（LCDAs），以规避欧几里得格点中光锥威尔逊线难以处理的问题；在施温格模型中对不同费米子质量进行了数值验证，并系统量化了格点间距、体积、纠缠截断（bond dimension）、规范场截断及Trotter步长等带来的系统误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDFs和LCDAs作为非微扰强子结构的关键输入，在欧氏格点QCD中因光锥方向威尔逊线无法直接定义而难以获取，亟需Minkowski时空下的可行计算方案。

Method: 采用哈密顿量形式的光前量子化框架，将光锥威尔逊线实现为交替进行外部电荷空间平移与对矩阵乘积态（MPS）的Trotter化时间演化；通过调控bond dimension控制张量网络表达能力，并系统外推格点参数以量化系统误差。

Result: 在施温格模型中成功计算了费米子/反费米子PDFs、LCDAs及矢量介子衰变常数；验证了归一化条件、动量求和规则与对称性；证实纠缠熵增长缓慢，中等bond dimension已足够精确。

Conclusion: 该方法为在Minkowski时空下基于张量网络计算光前矩阵元提供了严格可行且系统可控的路径，具备向(3+1)维QCD推广的理论基础与数值可行性。

Abstract: Parton distribution functions (PDFs) and light-cone distribution amplitudes (LCDAs) encode the non-perturbative structure of hadrons in terms of their partonic constituents. They are defined through matrix elements containing parton operators that are connected by a Wilson line along a light-front direction, which makes them difficult to access in Euclidean lattice calculations. We demonstrate how parton functions can be directly obtained in Minkowski space using the Hamiltonian framework. We compute fermion and antifermion PDFs, LCDAs, and decay constants of the vector meson in the Schwinger model for several fermion masses. States in Hilbert space are efficiently represented by tensor networks, with an expressivity that is systematically controlled by a bond dimension. The light-front Wilson line is implemented as a stepwise evolution on the lattice that alternates spatial shifts of an external charge with Trotterized time-evolution steps acting on a matrix product state. All systematic uncertainties are quantified by varying and extrapolating in the lattice spacing, volume, bond dimension, gauge-field truncation, and Trotter step width. The slow growth of the entanglement entropy makes moderate bond dimensions sufficient, which supports the feasibility of using tensor networks for computing light-front matrix elements in more general settings. We confirm that the computed distributions fulfill the expected normalizations, momentum sum rules, and symmetry properties.

</details>


### [116] [Intrinsic Soft Function in the Continuum and Physical Mass Limit from Lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31810)
*Zhi-Chao Gong,Min-Huan Chu,Jun Hua,Xiangdong Ji,Andreas Schäfer,Jin-Xin Tan,Han-Zhang Wang,Wei Wang,Jian-Hui Zhang,Mu-Hua Zhang,Qi-An Zhang*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文首次在格点QCD中同时实现连续极限、物理π介子质量与无穷动量极限下，利用大动量有效理论（LaMET）从轻介子形状因子和准TMD波函数中提取本征软函数；通过幂次计数引导的轴矢量插值算符显著提升信噪比，实现了物理π质量下的直接计算，并给出横动量空间软函数在b⊥≈1.1 fm范围内的精确行为，其横距离依赖呈近似指数衰减，特征尺度Λ≈0.7 GeV（μ=2 GeV）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本征软函数是横动量依赖（TMD）因子化中普适的非微扰成分，但此前格点QCD研究未能同时控制连续极限、物理π质量与无穷动量极限，限制了其在高精度TMD解析中的应用。

Method: 采用大动量有效理论（LaMET），结合轻介子电磁形状因子与准TMD波函数的格点计算；引入幂次计数指导的轴矢量插值算符以压制高维算符污染并增强信号；系统分析六个不同格距（覆盖四个a值）的格点系综，外推至连续极限与物理π质量，并实施大动量外推以逼近无穷动量极限。

Result: 首次获得满足三重极限的本征软函数数值结果，横距离b⊥可达≈1.1 fm；其b⊥依赖呈近似指数衰减，拟合得有效红外尺度Λ≈0.7 GeV（重整化能标μ=2 GeV）；短距行为与微扰QCD预言一致，验证了LaMET框架下软函数的可提性与一致性。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了格点QCD计算本征软函数的完备方法论，为TMD因子化提供首个定量非微扰基准，显著推进了强子结构中Wilson线动力学的非微扰理解与精度前沿。

Abstract: The intrinsic soft function is a universal nonperturbative ingredient of transverse-momentum-dependent (TMD) factorization. Despite substantial progress in lattice QCD studies, a determination with simultaneous control of the continuum, physical-pion-mass, and infinite-momentum limits has remained unavailable. In this work we present the first lattice determination incorporating all these limits within large-momentum effective theory, extracting the intrinsic soft function from a light-meson form factor and quasi-TMD wave functions. A key advance is the use of power-counting-guided axial-vector interpolators, which substantially improve the signal and enable a direct calculation at the physical pion mass. Combined with results from six ensembles spanning four lattice spacings, this improvement allows a precise determination extending to $b_\perp\simeq1.1~\mathrm{fm}$, while remaining consistent with perturbative predictions at short distances. The resulting transverse-distance dependence exhibits an approximately exponential decay, with the decay rate being characterized by an effective scale $\Lambda\simeq0.7~\mathrm{GeV}$ at $\mu=2~\mathrm{GeV}$, providing a quantitative benchmark for nonperturbative Wilson-line dynamics and essential input for precision TMD studies.

</details>


### [117] [Bound-state preparation in strongly coupled theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33079)
*Scott Lawrence,Duff Neill*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类无需量子相位估计算法的复合态制备方法，利用弱耦合辅助场在强耦合理论（包括非阿贝尔规范理论）中制备渐近态（如束缚态），并通过一维非相对论理论的经典模拟验证，并估算其在QCD中J/ψ产生过程的应用效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强耦合量子场论中难以通过绝热演化从自由理论出发制备渐近态（如束缚态），而这类态是量子模拟（如散射计算）的关键输入。

Method: 引入弱耦合辅助场与目标强耦合系统相互作用，构造可控制演化的含参哈密顿量，通过变分优化或受控绝热演化实现目标希尔伯特子空间中最低激发态（即所需渐近态）的制备；全程避免量子相位估计。

Result: 在经典模拟的一维非相对论模型上成功实现了复合态制备；对QCD中J/ψ产生的产率进行了量化估算，表明该方法具有实际可行性。

Conclusion: 该辅助场方法为强耦合规范理论中的物理态制备提供了新范式，兼具理论普适性与实验可实现性，有望应用于NISQ时代量子模拟器上的高能物理问题。

Abstract: Many quantum simulation tasks, including scattering in quantum field theories and effective non-relativistic theories, require the preparation of asymptotic states. Such a state, which may be a bound state, is typically the lowest-lying excited state in some sector of Hilbert space. At weak coupling these states can often be prepared adiabatically by beginning with the free theory, but this is not generally feasible in strongly coupled theories. We detail a family of algorithms for preparing composite states in strongly coupled theories, including non-Abelian gauge theories, by using weakly coupled auxiliary fields. These methods do not require quantum phase estimation. We demonstrate the method numerically, via classical simulation, on nonrelativistic theories in one spatial dimension. We estimate the yield of this method applied to $J/\psi$ production in quantum chromodynamics.

</details>


### [118] [Prospects and limitations of adaptive variational quantum algorithms for lattice field theories: a case study in 2+1 dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33138)
*Nico Dichter,Andrea Bulgarelli,Lena Funcke,Stefan Kühn*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究ADAPT-VQE算法在高于1+1维格点规范场论中的适用性，揭示其两大根本限制：实哈密顿量与实初态导致实池算符梯度为零；算法易收敛至激发态。以2+1维自由Wilson费米子为例验证，并通过引入电荷共轭对称性算符池及组合不同初态与池，成功恢复基态与第一激发态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验ADAPT-VQE能否推广至更高维（≥2+1D）格点场论，因此前仅在1+1D真空制备中成功应用，而高维情形面临更复杂的希尔伯特空间结构与对称性约束。

Method: 理论分析ADAPT-VQE梯度消失机制与激发态收敛行为；数值模拟2+1D自由Wilson费米子模型；构造含电荷共轭对称性的扩展算符池；采用多初态-多池组合策略进行变分优化。

Result: 证实ADAPT-VQE在实哈密顿量/实初态下因时间反演对称性导致实池算符梯度恒为零；观察到算法在质量参数扫描中系统性收敛至激发态；引入C对称性池并结合合适初态后，可在全质量范围内复现基态与第一激发态能量及关联函数。

Conclusion: ADAPT-VQE直接迁移至高维格点场论存在内在障碍，须通过算符池对称性增强与初态协同设计来克服；该工作为后续在QCD等相互作用理论中部署自适应变分算法提供了关键准则。

Abstract: The variational quantum algorithm ADAPT-VQE has previously been used to prepare the vacuum of lattice gauge theories in $1+1D$. In this paper, we study whether it extends to higher dimensions. We identify two general limitations of ADAPT-VQE. First, if the qubit Hamiltonian and the initial state are real, i.e., invariant under qubit-level time-reversal, all real-valued pool operators have vanishing gradients and are never selected. Second, the algorithm can converge to excited eigenstates. We illustrate both limitations, also providing examples from literature, and present a first application of ADAPT-VQE to a lattice field theory in $2+1D$, namely free Wilson fermions, where both obstacles occur. Adding charge conjugation symmetry to the operator pool improves the results, and combining different pools with suitable initial states recovers the ground and first excited states over the full mass range. These findings give practical guidelines for applying ADAPT-VQE to lattice field theories beyond $1+1D$.

</details>
